========== K41 LAUNCHER SOURCE: REPOSITORY ==========
size=1075 mode=-rw------- uid=1000 gid=1000 modified=2026-11-12 12:14:31.795993723 +0700
7057e56712b467e2a7a063e41bf3dfb50dce9a2ed836da15def598816868aa06  <HOME>/Lenovo-ThinkPad-X280-K41/stages/stage-5-desktop-customization/workstreams/01-boot-experience/scripts/k41-start-hyprland.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Launch upstream start-hyprland without printing its startup output on tty1.
# stdin and the controlling TTY remain attached; stdout/stderr are retained privately.
set -Eeuo pipefail
umask 077

die() { printf 'K41 Hyprland launcher: %s\n' "$*" >&2; exit 1; }

launcher="$(command -v start-hyprland || true)"
[[ -n $launcher && -x $launcher ]] || die 'start-hyprland is not available in PATH.'
[[ $launcher != "$HOME/.local/bin/k41-start-hyprland" ]] || die 'Refusing recursive launcher resolution.'

state_dir="$HOME/.local/state/k41"
log_file="$state_dir/hyprland-startup.log"
install -d -m 0700 "$state_dir"
[[ ! -L $state_dir && -d $state_dir ]] || die 'The K41 state directory must be a real directory, not a symlink.'
[[ ! -L $log_file ]] || die 'Refusing to write through a symlink log file.'

if [[ -e $log_file ]]; then
    [[ -f $log_file ]] || die 'The startup log path is not a regular file.'
    if [[ $(stat -c %s -- "$log_file") -ge 1048576 ]]; then
        mv -f -- "$log_file" "$log_file.1"
    fi
fi

exec "$launcher" >>"$log_file" 2>&1

========== BASH PROFILE K41 AUTOLOGIN BLOCK ==========
# K41 HYPRLAND AUTOLOGIN START
# Start only on tty1; other TTYs retain their normal password <REDACTED>
if [[ -z ${WAYLAND_DISPLAY:-} && -z ${DISPLAY:-} && -z ${K41_AUTOLOGIN_SESSION:-} ]] &&
   [[ "$(tty 2>/dev/null || true)" == /dev/tty1 ]] &&
   command -v start-hyprland >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    export K41_AUTOLOGIN_SESSION=1
    mkdir -p "$HOME/.cache/k41"
    rm -f -- "$HOME/.cache/k41/boot-lock-ready"
    "$HOME/.local/bin/k41-start-hyprland"
fi
# K41 HYPRLAND AUTOLOGIN END

========== INSTALLED LOCK HELPER SOURCE (redacted) ==========
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Start and verify the Caelestia session lock before publishing the Plymouth handoff marker.
# Hyprlock fallback uses a repository-managed config passed explicitly with --config.
set -Eeuo pipefail
umask 077

die() {
    printf 'K41 lock startup: ERROR: %s\n' "$*" >&2
    exit 1
}
log() {
    printf '[%s] %s\n' "$(date -u +'%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')" "$*"
}

(( EUID != 0 )) || die 'Run as the regular desktop user, never with sudo.'
[[ -n ${HOME:-} && $HOME == /* && -d $HOME ]] || die 'HOME is missing or invalid.'

for command_name in caelestia date chmod mkdir mktemp mv sleep stat timeout grep kill; do
    command -v "$command_name" >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "Required command is unavailable: $command_name"
done

state_root="$HOME/.local/state"
k41_state="$state_root/k41"
log_root="$k41_state/lockscreen"
for path in "$HOME/.local" "$state_root" "$k41_state" "$log_root"; do
    [[ ! -L $path ]] || die "Refusing symlinked state path: $path"
    [[ ! -e $path || -d $path ]] || die "State path is not a directory: $path"
    if [[ -e $path ]]; then
        [[ $(stat -c %u -- "$path") == "$EUID" ]] || die "State path is not owned by the desktop user: $path"
    fi
done
mkdir -p -- "$log_root"
[[ -d $log_root && ! -L $log_root && $(stat -c %u -- "$log_root") == "$EUID" ]] ||
    die 'Cannot establish a user-owned lock startup log directory.'

stamp="$(date -u +'%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')"
log_file="$log_root/startup-$stamp-${BASHPID}.log"
[[ ! -e $log_file && ! -L $log_file ]] || die "Refusing to overwrite existing log: $log_file"
( set -o noclobber; : >"$log_file" ) 2>/dev/null || die "Cannot create startup log: $log_file"
chmod 0600 "$log_file"
exec >>"$log_file" 2>&1

log "Lock startup helper started."
log "Readiness budget: ${K41_LOCK_READY_TIMEOUT:-30}s; verification attempts: ${K41_LOCK_VERIFY_RETRIES:-10}."

ready_timeout=${K41_LOCK_READY_TIMEOUT:-30}
interval=${K41_LOCK_INTERVAL:-1}
verify_attempts=${K41_LOCK_VERIFY_RETRIES:-10}
command_timeout=1

[[ $ready_timeout =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]] || die 'K41_LOCK_READY_TIMEOUT must be a positive integer.'
(( ready_timeout <= 30 )) || die 'K41_LOCK_READY_TIMEOUT must not exceed 30 seconds.'
[[ $interval =~ ^[01]$ ]] || die 'K41_LOCK_INTERVAL must be 0 or 1 second.'
[[ $verify_attempts =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]] || die 'K41_LOCK_VERIFY_RETRIES must be a positive integer.'
(( verify_attempts <= 10 )) || die 'K41_LOCK_VERIFY_RETRIES must not exceed 10 attempts.'

marker_dir="$HOME/.cache/k41"
for path in "$HOME/.cache" "$marker_dir"; do
    [[ ! -L $path ]] || die "Refusing symlinked marker path: $path"
    [[ ! -e $path || -d $path ]] || die "Marker path is not a directory: $path"
    if [[ -e $path ]]; then
        [[ $(stat -c %u -- "$path") == "$EUID" ]] || die "Marker path is not owned by the desktop user: $path"
    fi
done
mkdir -p -- "$marker_dir"
[[ -d $marker_dir && ! -L $marker_dir && $(stat -c %u -- "$marker_dir") == "$EUID" ]] ||
    die 'Cannot establish a user-owned readiness marker directory.'

marker="$marker_dir/boot-lock-ready"
if [[ -e $marker || -L $marker ]]; then
    [[ -f $marker && ! -L $marker && $(stat -c %u -- "$marker") == "$EUID" ]] ||
        die 'Existing readiness marker is not a regular user-owned file.'
fi

mark_ready() {
    local temporary
    temporary="$(mktemp "$marker_dir/.boot-lock-ready.XXXXXX")" ||
        die 'Cannot create temporary readiness marker.'
    chmod 0600 "$temporary"
    mv -f -- "$temporary" "$marker"
    log 'Readiness marker published.'
}

last_ipc_output=''
lock_ipc_ready() {
    local listing
    if ! listing="$(timeout --signal=TERM --kill-after=1 "${command_timeout}s" caelestia shell -s 2>&1)"; then
        last_ipc_output=$listing
        return 1
    fi
    if grep -Eq 'target[[:space:]]+lock([[:space:]]|$)' <<<"$listing" &&
       grep -Eq 'function[[:space:]]+isLocked\(\):[[:space:]]*bool' <<<"$listing"; then
        return 0
    fi
    last_ipc_output=$listing
    return 1
}

is_locked() {
    local state
    state="$(timeout --signal=TERM --kill-after=1 "${command_timeout}s" caelestia shell lock isLocked 2>&1)" ||
        return 1
    [[ $state =~ ^[[:space:]]*true[[:space:]]*$ ]]
}

# Discovery and verification are separate bounded phases. No nested retry multiplication.
deadline=$((SECONDS + ready_timeout))
ipc_ready=0
while (( SECONDS < deadline )); do
    if lock_ipc_ready; then
        ipc_ready=1
        log 'Caelestia lock IPC is ready.'
        break
    fi
    sleep "$interval"
done

if (( ipc_ready == 1 )); then
    if timeout --signal=TERM --kill-after=1 "${command_timeout}s" caelestia shell lock lock; then
        log 'Caelestia lock request accepted.'
        for ((attempt = 1; attempt <= verify_attempts; attempt++)); do
            if is_locked; then
                log "Caelestia lock state verified on attempt $attempt."
                mark_ready
                exit 0
            fi
            log "Caelestia lock state not yet verified ($attempt/$verify_attempts)."
            sleep "$interval"
        done
        log 'Caelestia lock did not reach verified state within the bounded verification window.'
    else
        log 'Caelestia lock IPC rejected or timed out on the lock request.'
    fi
else
    log "Caelestia lock IPC did not become ready within ${ready_timeout}s."
    if [[ -n $last_ipc_output ]]; then
        log "Last Caelestia IPC response: ${last_ipc_output:0:1200}"
    else
        log 'Caelestia IPC returned no diagnostic output.'
    fi
fi

# The fallback must use a known, managed config; never depend on an implicit search path.
if ! command -v hyprlock >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    die 'Caelestia lock was not verified and Hyprlock fallback is unavailable.'
fi
hyprlock_config="$HOME/.config/k41/hyprlock-fallback.conf"
[[ -f $hyprlock_config && ! -L $hyprlock_config && -s $hyprlock_config ]] ||
    die "Managed Hyprlock fallback config is missing or unsafe: $hyprlock_config"
[[ $(stat -c %u -- "$hyprlock_config") == "$EUID" ]] ||
    die 'Managed Hyprlock fallback config is not owned by the desktop user.'

log "Starting Hyprlock fallback with explicit config: $hyprlock_config"
hyprlock --config "$hyprlock_config" &
lock_pid=$!
sleep 1
if ! jobs -pr | grep -Fxq "$lock_pid"; then
    set +e
    wait "$lock_pid"
    fallback_status=$?
    set -e
    die "Hyprlock exited before readiness could be established (exit=$fallback_status)."
fi

mark_ready
log "Hyprlock fallback is running (pid=$lock_pid); waiting for unlock/exit."
set +e
wait "$lock_pid"
fallback_status=$?
set -e
log "Hyprlock fallback exited (exit=$fallback_status)."
exit "$fallback_status"

4225163b813cd9ff9cf093b917c6f4eedc474c00e6157f31e05b6721d13429ed  <HOME>/.local/state/k41/lockscreen/helper-backups/k41-lock-caelestia-after-start-20261111T073902Z-1845.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Wait for Caelestia's lock IPC, request a lock, verify it, then dismiss K41 overlay.
set -Eeuo pipefail

die() { printf 'K41 Caelestia lock: %s\n' "$*" >&2; exit 1; }
mark_ready() {
    local marker="$HOME/.cache/k41/boot-lock-ready"
    install -d -m 0700 "$(dirname -- "$marker")"
    : >"$marker"
    chmod 0600 "$marker"
}
dismiss_splash() {
    local pid_file="$HOME/.cache/k41/boot-splash.pid" pid
    if [[ -f $pid_file && ! -L $pid_file ]]; then
        IFS= read -r pid <"$pid_file" || true
        if [[ $pid =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]] && kill -0 "$pid" 2>/dev/null; then
            kill "$pid" 2>/dev/null || true
        fi
        rm -f -- "$pid_file"
    fi
}
command -v caelestia >/dev/null 2>&1 || die 'caelestia CLI is unavailable.'
command -v sleep >/dev/null 2>&1 || die 'sleep is unavailable.'
command -v grep >/dev/null 2>&1 || die 'grep is unavailable.'

attempts=${K41_LOCK_RETRIES:-45}
interval=${K41_LOCK_INTERVAL:-1}
verify_attempts=${K41_LOCK_VERIFY_RETRIES:-10}
[[ $attempts =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]] || die 'K41_LOCK_RETRIES must be a positive integer.'
[[ $interval =~ ^[0-9]+([.][0-9]+)?$ ]] || die 'K41_LOCK_INTERVAL must be numeric.'
[[ $verify_attempts =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]] || die 'K41_LOCK_VERIFY_RETRIES must be a positive integer.'

lock_ipc_ready() {
    caelestia shell -s 2>/dev/null |
        grep -Eq 'target[[:space:]]+lock([[:space:]]|$)' &&
    caelestia shell -s 2>/dev/null |
        grep -Eq 'function[[:space:]]+isLocked\(\):[[:space:]]*bool'
}
is_locked() {
    local state
    state=$(caelestia shell lock isLocked 2>/dev/null) || return 1
    [[ $state =~ ^[[:space:]]*true[[:space:]]*$ ]]
}
for ((i = 1; i <= attempts; i++)); do
    if lock_ipc_ready && caelestia shell lock lock; then
        for ((v = 1; v <= verify_attempts; v++)); do
            if is_locked; then
                printf 'K41 Caelestia lock: lock state verified.\n'
                dismiss_splash
                mark_ready
                exit 0
            fi
            sleep "$interval"
        done
    fi
    sleep "$interval"
done

printf 'K41 Caelestia lock: ERROR: lock IPC did not reach a verified locked state; starting Hyprlock fallback.\n' >&2
if command -v hyprlock >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    hyprlock &
    lock_pid=$!
    sleep 1
    if kill -0 "$lock_pid" 2>/dev/null; then
        dismiss_splash
        mark_ready
        wait "$lock_pid"
        exit $?
    fi
    wait "$lock_pid" || true
fi
die 'Caelestia lock could not be verified and Hyprlock fallback is unavailable.'

pragma ComponentBehavior: Bound

import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io
import Quickshell.Wayland
import qs.components.misc

Scope {
    property alias lock: lock

    WlSessionLock {
        id: lock

        signal unlock

        LockSurface {
            lock: lock
            pam: pam
        }
    }

    Pam {
        id: pam

        lock: lock
    }

    Loader {
        asynchronous: true
        active: true
        onLoaded: active = false

        // Force a load of a screencopy so the one in the lock works
        // My guess is the ICC backend loads async on first request, which if the lock is
        // the first request it fails to capture (because it's async and the compositor
        // refuses capture when locked)
        sourceComponent: ScreencopyView {
            captureSource: Quickshell.screens[0]
        }
    }

    // qmllint disable unresolved-type
    CustomShortcut {
        // qmllint enable unresolved-type
        name: "lock"
        description: "Lock the current session"
        onPressed: lock.locked = true
    }

    // qmllint disable unresolved-type
    CustomShortcut {
        // qmllint enable unresolved-type
        name: "unlock"
        description: "Unlock the current session"
        onPressed: lock.unlock()
    }

    IpcHandler {
        function lock(): void {
            lock.locked = true;
        }

        function unlock(): void {
            lock.unlock();
        }

        function isLocked(): bool {
            return lock.locked;
        }

        target: "lock"
    }
}

            path: Wallpapers.current
        }
    }

    Item {
        id: lockContent

        readonly property int size: lockIcon.implicitHeight + Tokens.padding.large * 4
        readonly property int radius: size / 4 * Tokens.rounding.scale

        anchors.centerIn: parent
        implicitWidth: size
        implicitHeight: size

        visible: Config.lock.enabled
        rotation: 180
        scale: 0

        // Remove the large surface enclosing the clock, date and password.
        // The individual password <REDACTED> retains its own rounded surface/border.
        StyledRect {
            id: lockBg

            anchors.fill: parent
            color: "transparent"
            radius: parent.radius
            opacity: 0
            layer.enabled: false
        }

        MaterialIcon {
            id: lockIcon

            anchors.centerIn: parent
            text: "lock"
            fontStyle: Tokens.font.icon.builders.extraLarge.scale(4).weight(Font.Bold).build()
            rotation: 180
        }

        Content {
            id: content

            anchors.centerIn: parent
            width: (root.screen?.height ?? 0) * Tokens.sizes.lock.heightMult * Tokens.sizes.lock.ratio - Tokens.padding.extraLargeIncreased
            height: (root.screen?.height ?? 0) * Tokens.sizes.lock.heightMult - Tokens.padding.extraLargeIncreased

            lock: root
            opacity: 0
            scale: 0
        }
    }
}

// K41 Floating Password <REDACTED> composition.
// Components Clock, PasswordInput and StateMessage are upstream Caelestia components.
import "center"
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Caelestia.Config
import qs.components
import qs.services

Item {
    id: root

    required property var lock
    readonly property real centerScale: Math.min(1, (lock.screen?.height ?? 1440) / 1440)
    readonly property int centerWidth: Tokens.sizes.lock.centerWidth * centerScale

    // Center the complete clock/date/password <REDACTED> in the full available area.
    ColumnLayout {
        anchors.centerIn: parent
        width: root.centerWidth
        spacing: Tokens.spacing.largeIncreased

        Clock {
            Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
            centerScale: root.centerScale
        }

        StyledText {
            Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
            text: Time.format("dddd • d MMM").toUpperCase()
            color: Colours.palette.m3onSurface
            font: Tokens.font.title.builders.medium.weight(Font.DemiBold).build()
        }

        PasswordInput {
            Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
            centerScale: Math.max(0.8, root.centerScale)
            centerWidth: root.centerWidth
            lock: root.lock
        }

        StateMessage {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            pam: root.lock.pam
        }
    }
}

path=Lenovo-ThinkPad-X280-K41/stages/stage-5-desktop-customization/workstreams/02-lock-screen/modules/lockscreen/center/PasswordInput.qml
size=6207 mode=-rw------- uid=1000 gid=1000 modified=2026-11-12 12:14:31.797993723 +0700
6de47c845d8f29002a9be9a72991f4521a131391343105637121365fb4c31a4b  <HOME>/Lenovo-ThinkPad-X280-K41/stages/stage-5-desktop-customization/workstreams/02-lock-screen/modules/lockscreen/center/PasswordInput.qml
// K41 Floating Password <REDACTED> field styling.
// Keep upstream PAM, keyboard handling, focus behavior and input controls;
// preserve the password <REDACTED> own rounded surface and border.
pragma ComponentBehavior: Bound

import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts
import M3Shapes
import Caelestia.Config
import qs.components
import qs.components.controls
import qs.services

StyledRect {
    id: root

    required property real centerScale
    required property int centerWidth
    required property var lock

    implicitWidth: {
        const w = centerWidth * 0.8;
        return lock.pam.buffer ? w : Math.min(w, inputField.placeholderWidth + iconWrapper.implicitWidth + enterButton.implicitWidth + input.spacing * 2 + Tokens.padding.medium * 2);
    }
    implicitHeight: input.implicitHeight + Tokens.padding.small

    // Keep the password <REDACTED> own surface; only the surrounding group is borderless.
    color: Colours.tPalette.m3surfaceContainer
    radius: Tokens.rounding.full

    focus: true
    onActiveFocusChanged: {
        if (!activeFocus)
            forceActiveFocus();
    }

    Keys.onPressed: event => {
        if (root.lock.unlocking)
            return;

        if (event.key === Qt.Key_Enter || event.key === Qt.Key_Return)
            inputField.placeholder.animate = false;

        root.lock.pam.handleKey(event);
    }

    Behavior on implicitWidth {
        Anim {}
    }

    StateLayer {
        hoverEnabled: false
        cursorShape: Qt.IBeamCursor
        onClicked: parent.forceActiveFocus()
    }

    RowLayout {
        id: input

        anchors.fill: parent
        anchors.margins: Tokens.padding.extraSmall
        spacing: Tokens.spacing.medium

        Item {
            id: iconWrapper

            Layout.fillHeight: true
            implicitWidth: height

            AnimLoader {
                anchors.centerIn: parent
                anchors.verticalCenterOffset: sourceComponent === iconComp ? 1 : 0
                sourceComp: root.lock.pam.passwd.active || root.lock.pam.howdy.active ? loadingComp : iconComp
            }

            Component {
                id: iconComp

                MaterialIcon {
                    animate: true
                    text: {
                        if (inputField.showPassword)
                            return "visibility";
                        if (root.lock.pam.fprint.tries >= GlobalConfig.lock.maxFprintTries) {
                            if (root.lock.pam.howdy.canAttempt)
                                return "face";
                            return "fingerprint_off";
                        }
                        if (root.lock.pam.fprint.active)
                            return "fingerprint";
                        if (root.lock.pam.howdy.canAttempt)
                            return "face";
                        return "lock";
                    }
                    color: !root.lock.pam.howdy.canAttempt && root.lock.pam.fprint.tries >= GlobalConfig.lock.maxFprintTries ? Colours.palette.m3error : Colours.palette.m3onSurfaceVariant
                    fontStyle: Tokens.font.icon.builders.medium.scale(root.centerScale).build()
                    fill: text === "face" || text === "visibility"

                    StateLayer {
                        anchors.fill: undefined
                        anchors.centerIn: parent
                        implicitWidth: {
                            const w = parent.implicitHeight + Tokens.padding.small * 2;
                            return w + (w % 2);
                        }
                        implicitHeight: implicitWidth
                        radius: Tokens.rounding.full

                        onClicked: {
                            parent.animate = false;
                            inputField.showPassword = !inputField.showPassword;
                            parent.animate = true;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            Component {
                id: loadingComp

                LoadingIndicator {
                    implicitSize: iconWrapper.height - Tokens.padding.small * 2
                }
            }
        }

        InputField {
            id: inputField

            Layout.fillWidth: true
            Layout.fillHeight: true

            centerScale: root.centerScale
            pam: root.lock.pam
        }

        Item {
            id: enterButton

            implicitWidth: implicitHeight
            implicitHeight: {
                const h = enterIcon.implicitHeight + Tokens.padding.extraSmall * 2;
                return h % 2 === 0 ? h : h + 1;
            }

            MaterialShape {
                anchors.fill: parent

                color: root.lock.pam.buffer ? Colours.palette.m3primary : Colours.layer(Colours.palette.m3surfaceContainerHigh, 2)
                shape: root.lock.pam.buffer ? MaterialShape.Arrow : MaterialShape.Circle
                scale: !root.lock.pam.buffer ? 1 : mouse.pressed ? 0.6 : mouse.containsMouse ? 0.8 : 0.7
                rotation: 90

                Behavior on scale {
                    Anim {
                        type: Anim.FastSpatial
                    }
                }

                Behavior on color {
                    CAnim {}
                }

                MouseArea {
                    id: mouse

                    anchors.fill: parent
                    hoverEnabled: true
                    cursorShape: root.lock.pam.buffer ? Qt.PointingHandCursor : Qt.ArrowCursor
                    onClicked: root.lock.pam.buffer && root.lock.pam.passwd.start()
                }
            }

            MaterialIcon {
                id: enterIcon

                anchors.centerIn: parent
                text: "arrow_forward"
                color: Colours.palette.m3onSurfaceVariant
                fontStyle: Tokens.font.icon.builders.medium.scale(root.centerScale * 1.2).build()
                opacity: root.lock.pam.buffer ? 0 : 1

                Behavior on opacity {
                    Anim {
                        type: Anim.DefaultEffects
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

hl.bind(mainMod .. " + right", hl.dsp.focus({ direction = "right" }))
hl.bind(mainMod .. " + up",    hl.dsp.focus({ direction = "up" }))
hl.bind(mainMod .. " + down",  hl.dsp.focus({ direction = "down" }))

-- Switch workspaces with mainMod + [0-9]
-- Move active window to a workspace with mainMod + SHIFT + [0-9]
for i = 1, 10 do
    local key = i % 10 -- 10 maps to key 0
    hl.bind(mainMod .. " + " .. key,             hl.dsp.focus({ workspace = i}))
    hl.bind(mainMod .. " + SHIFT + " .. key,     hl.dsp.window.move({ workspace = i }))
end

hl.bind(mainMod .. " + mouse_up",   hl.dsp.focus({ workspace = "e-1" }))

========== IMPLEMENTATION CONFIG CONTENT: .local/bin/k41-lock-caelestia-after-start ==========
size=6600 mode=-rwxr-xr-x uid=1000 gid=1000 modified=2026-11-12 11:10:26.687295960 +0700
0f8f0402af2b83640a36d0fed0408ab7b7f4fa109fbbf6a6543610aa259f885a  <HOME>/.local/bin/k41-lock-caelestia-after-start
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Start and verify the Caelestia session lock before publishing the Plymouth handoff marker.
# Hyprlock fallback uses a repository-managed config passed explicitly with --config.
set -Eeuo pipefail
umask 077

die() {
    printf 'K41 lock startup: ERROR: %s\n' "$*" >&2
    exit 1
}
log() {
    printf '[%s] %s\n' "$(date -u +'%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')" "$*"
}

(( EUID != 0 )) || die 'Run as the regular desktop user, never with sudo.'
[[ -n ${HOME:-} && $HOME == /* && -d $HOME ]] || die 'HOME is missing or invalid.'

for command_name in caelestia date chmod mkdir mktemp mv sleep stat timeout grep kill; do
    command -v "$command_name" >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "Required command is unavailable: $command_name"
done

state_root="$HOME/.local/state"
k41_state="$state_root/k41"
log_root="$k41_state/lockscreen"
for path in "$HOME/.local" "$state_root" "$k41_state" "$log_root"; do
    [[ ! -L $path ]] || die "Refusing symlinked state path: $path"
    [[ ! -e $path || -d $path ]] || die "State path is not a directory: $path"
    if [[ -e $path ]]; then
        [[ $(stat -c %u -- "$path") == "$EUID" ]] || die "State path is not owned by the desktop user: $path"
    fi
done
mkdir -p -- "$log_root"
[[ -d $log_root && ! -L $log_root && $(stat -c %u -- "$log_root") == "$EUID" ]] ||
    die 'Cannot establish a user-owned lock startup log directory.'

stamp="$(date -u +'%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')"
log_file="$log_root/startup-$stamp-${BASHPID}.log"
[[ ! -e $log_file && ! -L $log_file ]] || die "Refusing to overwrite existing log: $log_file"
( set -o noclobber; : >"$log_file" ) 2>/dev/null || die "Cannot create startup log: $log_file"
chmod 0600 "$log_file"
exec >>"$log_file" 2>&1

log "Lock startup helper started."
log "Readiness budget: ${K41_LOCK_READY_TIMEOUT:-30}s; verification attempts: ${K41_LOCK_VERIFY_RETRIES:-10}."

ready_timeout=${K41_LOCK_READY_TIMEOUT:-30}
interval=${K41_LOCK_INTERVAL:-1}
verify_attempts=${K41_LOCK_VERIFY_RETRIES:-10}
command_timeout=1

[[ $ready_timeout =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]] || die 'K41_LOCK_READY_TIMEOUT must be a positive integer.'
(( ready_timeout <= 30 )) || die 'K41_LOCK_READY_TIMEOUT must not exceed 30 seconds.'
[[ $interval =~ ^[01]$ ]] || die 'K41_LOCK_INTERVAL must be 0 or 1 second.'
[[ $verify_attempts =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]] || die 'K41_LOCK_VERIFY_RETRIES must be a positive integer.'
(( verify_attempts <= 10 )) || die 'K41_LOCK_VERIFY_RETRIES must not exceed 10 attempts.'

marker_dir="$HOME/.cache/k41"
for path in "$HOME/.cache" "$marker_dir"; do
    [[ ! -L $path ]] || die "Refusing symlinked marker path: $path"
    [[ ! -e $path || -d $path ]] || die "Marker path is not a directory: $path"
    if [[ -e $path ]]; then
        [[ $(stat -c %u -- "$path") == "$EUID" ]] || die "Marker path is not owned by the desktop user: $path"
    fi
done
mkdir -p -- "$marker_dir"
[[ -d $marker_dir && ! -L $marker_dir && $(stat -c %u -- "$marker_dir") == "$EUID" ]] ||
    die 'Cannot establish a user-owned readiness marker directory.'

marker="$marker_dir/boot-lock-ready"
if [[ -e $marker || -L $marker ]]; then
    [[ -f $marker && ! -L $marker && $(stat -c %u -- "$marker") == "$EUID" ]] ||
        die 'Existing readiness marker is not a regular user-owned file.'
fi

mark_ready() {
    local temporary
    temporary="$(mktemp "$marker_dir/.boot-lock-ready.XXXXXX")" ||
        die 'Cannot create temporary readiness marker.'
    chmod 0600 "$temporary"
    mv -f -- "$temporary" "$marker"
    log 'Readiness marker published.'
}

last_ipc_output=''
lock_ipc_ready() {
    local listing
    if ! listing="$(timeout --signal=TERM --kill-after=1 "${command_timeout}s" caelestia shell -s 2>&1)"; then
        last_ipc_output=$listing
        return 1
    fi
    if grep -Eq 'target[[:space:]]+lock([[:space:]]|$)' <<<"$listing" &&
       grep -Eq 'function[[:space:]]+isLocked\(\):[[:space:]]*bool' <<<"$listing"; then
        return 0
    fi
    last_ipc_output=$listing
    return 1
}

is_locked() {
    local state
    state="$(timeout --signal=TERM --kill-after=1 "${command_timeout}s" caelestia shell lock isLocked 2>&1)" ||
        return 1
    [[ $state =~ ^[[:space:]]*true[[:space:]]*$ ]]
}

# Discovery and verification are separate bounded phases. No nested retry multiplication.
deadline=$((SECONDS + ready_timeout))
ipc_ready=0
while (( SECONDS < deadline )); do
    if lock_ipc_ready; then
        ipc_ready=1
        log 'Caelestia lock IPC is ready.'
        break
    fi
    sleep "$interval"
done

if (( ipc_ready == 1 )); then
    if timeout --signal=TERM --kill-after=1 "${command_timeout}s" caelestia shell lock lock; then
        log 'Caelestia lock request accepted.'
        for ((attempt = 1; attempt <= verify_attempts; attempt++)); do
            if is_locked; then
                log "Caelestia lock state verified on attempt $attempt."
                mark_ready
                exit 0
            fi
            log "Caelestia lock state not yet verified ($attempt/$verify_attempts)."
            sleep "$interval"
        done
        log 'Caelestia lock did not reach verified state within the bounded verification window.'
    else
        log 'Caelestia lock IPC rejected or timed out on the lock request.'
    fi
else
    log "Caelestia lock IPC did not become ready within ${ready_timeout}s."
    if [[ -n $last_ipc_output ]]; then
        log "Last Caelestia IPC response: ${last_ipc_output:0:1200}"
    else
        log 'Caelestia IPC returned no diagnostic output.'
    fi
fi

# The fallback must use a known, managed config; never depend on an implicit search path.
if ! command -v hyprlock >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    die 'Caelestia lock was not verified and Hyprlock fallback is unavailable.'
fi
hyprlock_config="$HOME/.config/k41/hyprlock-fallback.conf"
[[ -f $hyprlock_config && ! -L $hyprlock_config && -s $hyprlock_config ]] ||
    die "Managed Hyprlock fallback config is missing or unsafe: $hyprlock_config"
[[ $(stat -c %u -- "$hyprlock_config") == "$EUID" ]] ||
    die 'Managed Hyprlock fallback config is not owned by the desktop user.'

log "Starting Hyprlock fallback with explicit config: $hyprlock_config"
hyprlock --config "$hyprlock_config" &
lock_pid=$!
sleep 1
if ! jobs -pr | grep -Fxq "$lock_pid"; then
    set +e
    wait "$lock_pid"
    fallback_status=$?
    set -e
    die "Hyprlock exited before readiness could be established (exit=$fallback_status)."
fi

mark_ready
log "Hyprlock fallback is running (pid=$lock_pid); waiting for unlock/exit."
set +e
wait "$lock_pid"
fallback_status=$?
set -e
log "Hyprlock fallback exited (exit=$fallback_status)."
exit "$fallback_status"

========== BOOT HANDOFF UNIT CONFIGURATION (selected directives only) ==========

-- /etc/systemd/system/plymouth-quit.service.d/k41-lock-handoff.conf --
2:After=getty@tty1.service
5:ExecStart=
6:ExecStart=/usr/local/libexec/k41-plymouth-quit-after-lock "<HOME>/.cache/k41/boot-lock-ready"
7:TimeoutStartSec=85

-- /etc/systemd/system/k41-plymouth-release.service --
2:Description=Retain splash frame and release Plymouth display ownership before K41 Hyprland startup
3:Wants=plymouth-start.service
4:After=plymouth-start.service
5:Before=getty@tty1.service
8:Type=oneshot
9:ExecStartPre=/usr/bin/rm -f -- "<HOME>/.cache/k41/boot-lock-ready"
10:ExecStart=-/usr/bin/plymouth quit --retain-splash
14:WantedBy=multi-user.target

========== LOG FILE: .local/state/k41/lockscreen/startup-audits/20261112T054537Z/README.txt ==========
K41 desktop startup diagnostic
===============================
Run this script as the regular desktop user from tty2 or a normal shell:

  bash stages/stage-5-desktop-customization/workstreams/02-lock-screen/scripts/k41-audit-desktop-startup.sh

Report: report.txt

The collector does not modify configuration, packages, services, authentication, or boot state. By default it does not launch a shell. To run the optional 15-second foreground Quickshell diagnostic from an unlocked terminal inside the active Hyprland session, use:
  
  K41_AUDIT_RUN_QS_PROBE=1 bash stages/stage-5-desktop-customization/workstreams/02-lock-screen/scripts/k41-audit-desktop-startup.sh

The probe is skipped if Wayland session variables/socket are missing or a Quickshell/Caelestia process is already running. It may briefly display shell windows. The local report is stored privately outside the Git checkout at ~/.local/state/k41/lockscreen/startup-audits/; only a redacted copy is published.
The script publishes a sanitized copy of this run directory (report.txt, README.txt, lifecycle-timeline.tsv) to GitHub main using existing Git credentials. The local report remains private (0600); if publication fails, the report stays local and the script exits non-zero. Review the published report for any residual private information.

========== DIAGNOSTIC LIMITATIONS ==========
- Collector tidak menjalankan start-hyprland, Hyprland, Caelestia reload, atau lock command secara default.
- Opt-in K41_AUDIT_RUN_QS_PROBE=1 menjalankan qs -c caelestia secara foreground maksimal 15 detik untuk menangkap QML/plugin/import error; probe dilewati jika session Wayland tidak valid atau Quickshell sudah berjalan.
- Collector tidak mengubah service, package, bootloader, initramfs, TPM/LUKS, PAM, permission, ownership, maupun konfigurasi.
- Hyprlock auth/unlock lines are parsed from helper-captured stdout/stderr; if upstream emits no timestamp, the timeline records UNKNOWN rather than inventing one.
- Journal-derived PAM/auth signals remain separate from Hyprlock helper-log signals; absence in one source does not negate evidence in the other.
- Journal mungkin tidak dapat dibaca lengkap oleh user biasa; permission-denied dicatat sebagai keterbatasan.
- Report memuat QML lockscreen repository/runtime yang dipilih, potongan log, metadata, dan hash. Redaksi otomatis tidak menjamin seluruh data pribadi terhapus.
- Salinan report tersanitasi dipublikasikan otomatis; redaksi tidak menjamin seluruh data privat terhapus, jadi tinjau report di GitHub dan hapus data sensitif bila ditemukan.
- TTY2 fallback dapat merupakan konsekuensi eksplisit dari k41-plymouth-quit-after-lock.sh setelah timeout 75 detik; bukti journal menentukan apakah mekanisme itu yang berjalan.
Local report: ~/.local/state/k41/lockscreen/startup-audits/20261112T054537Z/report.txt

========== DIAGNOSTIC LIMITATIONS ==========
- Collector tidak menjalankan start-hyprland, Hyprland, Caelestia reload, atau lock command secara default.
- Opt-in K41_AUDIT_RUN_QS_PROBE=1 menjalankan qs -c caelestia secara foreground maksimal 15 detik untuk menangkap QML/plugin/import error; probe dilewati jika session Wayland tidak valid atau Quickshell sudah berjalan.
- Collector tidak mengubah service, package, bootloader, initramfs, TPM/LUKS, PAM, permission, ownership, maupun konfigurasi.
- Hyprlock auth/unlock lines are parsed from helper-captured stdout/stderr; if upstream emits no timestamp, the timeline records UNKNOWN rather than inventing one.
- Journal-derived PAM/auth signals remain separate from Hyprlock helper-log signals; absence in one source does not negate evidence in the other.
- Journal mungkin tidak dapat dibaca lengkap oleh user biasa; permission-denied dicatat sebagai keterbatasan.
- Report memuat QML lockscreen repository/runtime yang dipilih, potongan log, metadata, dan hash. Redaksi otomatis tidak menjamin seluruh data pribadi terhapus.
- Salinan report tersanitasi dipublikasikan otomatis; redaksi tidak menjamin seluruh data privat terhapus, jadi tinjau report di GitHub dan hapus data sensitif bila ditemukan.
- TTY2 fallback dapat merupakan konsekuensi eksplisit dari k41-plymouth-quit-after-lock.sh setelah timeout 75 detik; bukti journal menentukan apakah mekanisme itu yang berjalan.

========== LOG FILE: .local/state/k41/lockscreen/startup-audits/20261112T060827Z/README.txt ==========
K41 desktop startup diagnostic
===============================
Run this script as the regular desktop user from tty2 or a normal shell:

  bash stages/stage-5-desktop-customization/workstreams/02-lock-screen/scripts/k41-audit-desktop-startup.sh

Report: report.txt

The collector does not modify configuration, packages, services, authentication, or boot state. By default it does not launch a shell. To run the optional 15-second foreground Quickshell diagnostic from an unlocked terminal inside the active Hyprland session, use:
  
  K41_AUDIT_RUN_QS_PROBE=1 bash stages/stage-5-desktop-customization/workstreams/02-lock-screen/scripts/k41-audit-desktop-startup.sh

The probe is skipped if Wayland session variables/socket are missing or a Quickshell/Caelestia process is already running. It may briefly display shell windows. The local report is stored privately outside the Git checkout at ~/.local/state/k41/lockscreen/startup-audits/; only a redacted copy is published.
The script publishes a sanitized copy of this run directory (report.txt, README.txt, lifecycle-timeline.tsv) to GitHub main using existing Git credentials. The local report remains private (0600); if publication fails, the report stays local and the script exits non-zero. Review the published report for any residual private information.

========== DIAGNOSTIC LIMITATIONS ==========
- Collector tidak menjalankan start-hyprland, Hyprland, Caelestia reload, atau lock command secara default.
- Opt-in K41_AUDIT_RUN_QS_PROBE=1 menjalankan qs -c caelestia secara foreground maksimal 15 detik untuk menangkap QML/plugin/import error; probe dilewati jika session Wayland tidak valid atau Quickshell sudah berjalan.
- Collector tidak mengubah service, package, bootloader, initramfs, TPM/LUKS, PAM, permission, ownership, maupun konfigurasi.
- Hyprlock auth/unlock lines are parsed from helper-captured stdout/stderr; if upstream emits no timestamp, the timeline records UNKNOWN rather than inventing one.
- Journal-derived PAM/auth signals remain separate from Hyprlock helper-log signals; absence in one source does not negate evidence in the other.
- Journal mungkin tidak dapat dibaca lengkap oleh user biasa; permission-denied dicatat sebagai keterbatasan.
- Report memuat QML lockscreen repository/runtime yang dipilih, potongan log, metadata, dan hash. Redaksi otomatis tidak menjamin seluruh data pribadi terhapus.
- Salinan report tersanitasi dipublikasikan otomatis; redaksi tidak menjamin seluruh data privat terhapus, jadi tinjau report di GitHub dan hapus data sensitif bila ditemukan.
- TTY2 fallback dapat merupakan konsekuensi eksplisit dari k41-plymouth-quit-after-lock.sh setelah timeout 75 detik; bukti journal menentukan apakah mekanisme itu yang berjalan.

===== NORMALIZED LOCK LIFECYCLE TIMELINE =====
Evidence status is literal: NOT_OBSERVED does not mean the event did not happen.
HYPRLOCK_AUTH_SUCCESS/HYPRLOCK_AUTH_FAILURE/HYPRLOCK_UNLOCK_CONFIRMED are parsed from helper-captured Hyprlock stdout/stderr; timestamp is UNKNOWN when upstream output has no timestamp.
AUTH_SUCCESS/AUTH_FAILURE/UNLOCK_EVENT remain separate journal-derived signals and may be absent because PAM logging is implementation-dependent.
LOCK_SURFACE_VISIBLE and DESKTOP_RESTORED_AFTER_UNLOCK are NOT_INSTRUMENTED, not NOT_OBSERVED; no reliable correlated event source exists yet.

========== DIAGNOSTIC LIMITATIONS ==========
- Collector tidak menjalankan start-hyprland, Hyprland, Caelestia reload, atau lock command secara default.
- Opt-in K41_AUDIT_RUN_QS_PROBE=1 menjalankan qs -c caelestia secara foreground maksimal 15 detik untuk menangkap QML/plugin/import error; probe dilewati jika session Wayland tidak valid atau Quickshell sudah berjalan.
- Collector tidak mengubah service, package, bootloader, initramfs, TPM/LUKS, PAM, permission, ownership, maupun konfigurasi.
- Hyprlock auth/unlock lines are parsed from helper-captured stdout/stderr; if upstream emits no timestamp, the timeline records UNKNOWN rather than inventing one.
- Journal-derived PAM/auth signals remain separate from Hyprlock helper-log signals; absence in one source does not negate evidence in the other.
- Journal mungkin tidak dapat dibaca lengkap oleh user biasa; permission-denied dicatat sebagai keterbatasan.
- Report memuat QML lockscreen repository/runtime yang dipilih, potongan log, metadata, dan hash. Redaksi otomatis tidak menjamin seluruh data pribadi terhapus.
- Salinan report tersanitasi dipublikasikan otomatis; redaksi tidak menjamin seluruh data privat terhapus, jadi tinjau report di GitHub dan hapus data sensitif bila ditemukan.
- TTY2 fallback dapat merupakan konsekuensi eksplisit dari k41-plymouth-quit-after-lock.sh setelah timeout 75 detik; bukti journal menentukan apakah mekanisme itu yang berjalan.
Local report: ~/.local/state/k41/lockscreen/startup-audits/20261112T061706Z/report.txt
